We validate the proposed indicators on several synthetic confidence distributions under multiple controlled calibration profiles and on fifteen real datasets with and without post-hoc calibration. Experiments demonstrate that CSR achieves near-perfect sensitivity and specificity across all tested conditions.

</details>


### [67] [Federated Semi-Supervised Graph Neural Networks with Prototype-Guided Pseudo-Labeling for Privacy-Preserving Gestational Diabetes Mellitus Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01810)
*G. Victor Daniela,A. Mallikarjuna Reddya,Uday Kumar Addankia,Sridhar Reddy Gogua,Sravanth Kumar Ramakuria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedTGNN-SS，一种隐私保护的联邦半监督框架，通过k-NN图构建、原型引导伪标签、自适应图优化、临床感知数据增强和隐私安全原型共享，解决GDM等妊娠期糖尿病早期筛查中的标签稀缺和数据隐私双重挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 妊娠期糖尿病(GDM)发病率高，需要早期准确的风险分层。但实际临床部署机器学习面临两个耦合约束：(i)大量电子健康记录缺乏确诊标签(标签稀缺)，(ii)跨医院患者级数据共享存在隐私限制(数据隐私)。现有方法无法同时解决这两个问题。

Method: (1) 每个医院构建局部k-NN患者相似度图并训练拓扑自适应GNN编码器；(2) 原型引导伪标签与邻域一致性；(3) 自适应图精炼，利用学习到的嵌入周期性更新k-NN图；(4) 仅对连续变量进行临床感知一致性增强；(5) 仅交换类级质心的隐私安全原型共享。

Result: 在3个糖尿病数据集(GDM: 3,525例; Pima: 768例; Early Stage: 520例)上，每节点10%-80%标签缺失条件下，与11个联邦基线相比取得56次显著胜出(p<0.05)；极端稀缺下表现强劲(Pima: 80%缺失时AUROC 0.8037; Early Stage: 80%缺失时AUROC 0.9634)。

Conclusion: FedTGNN-SS有效结合联邦学习与半监督学习，在保护数据隐私的同时充分利用未标注数据，为临床表格型EHR的GDM早期风险分层提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Gestational Diabetes Mellitus (GDM) is a high-prevalence pregnancy complication that requires accurate early risk stratification to reduce maternal and fetal morbidity. However, real-world clinical deployment of machine learning is hindered by two coupled constraints: (i) label scarcity, where a large fraction of electronic health records (EHR) lack confirmed diagnostic labels, and (ii) data privacy, which prevents sharing patient-level data across hospitals. This paper proposes FedTGNN-SS, a privacy-preserving federated semi-supervised framework for clinical tabular EHR. Each hospital builds a local k-nearest-neighbor patient similarity graph and trains a topology-adaptive GNN encoder. To robustly exploit unlabeled records, FedTGNN-SS combines (1) prototype-guided pseudo-labeling with neighborhood agreement, (2) adaptive graph refinement that periodically updates the k-NN graph using learned embeddings, (3) clinical-aware consistency augmentation applied only to continuous variables, and (4) privacy-safe prototype sharing that exchanges only class-level centroids. Across three diabetes-related datasets (GDM: N = 3,525; Pima: N = 768; Early Stage: N = 520) under 10\%-80\% missing labels per silo, FedTGNN-SS achieves 56 significant wins ($p < 0.05$) against 11 federated baselines and attains strong AUROC under extreme scarcity (Pima: 0.8037 at 80\% missing, Early Stage: 0.9634 at 80\% missing).

</details>


### [68] [Skipping the Zeros in Diffusion Models for Sparse Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01817)
*Phil Sidney Ostheimer,Mayank Nagda,Andriy Balinskyy,Gabriel Vicente Rodrigues,Jean Radig,Carl Herrmann,Stephan Mandt,Marius Kloft,Sophie Fellenz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散模型无法有效处理稀疏连续数据（如大量零值）的问题，提出Sparsity-Exploiting Diffusion (SED)方法，通过对非零值建模并在训练和推理中跳过零值，在保持或提升生成质量的同时实现计算节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型擅长处理稠密连续数据，但无法建模稀疏连续数据中的精确零值（代表信号的有意缺失），导致稀疏模式被抹除且在零值上进行不必要的计算。

Method: 仅对非零值进行建模，在训练和推理过程中跳过零值计算，保留数据的稀疏性。

Result: 在物理和生物学基准上匹配或超越传统扩散模型及领域特定基线，视觉实验揭示了稠密扩散模型的局限性及SED的优势。

Conclusion: SED能够在不牺牲生成质量的前提下实现计算效率提升，为处理稀疏连续数据提供有效方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models (DMs) excel on dense continuous data, but are not designed for sparse continuous data. They do not model exact zeros that represent the deliberate absence of a signal. As a result, they erase sparsity patterns and perform unnecessary computation on mostly zero entries. With Sparsity-Exploiting Diffusion (SED), we model only non-zero values, preserving sparsity. SED delivers computational savings while maintaining or improving generation quality by skipping zeros during training and inference. Across physics and biology benchmarks, SED matches or surpasses conventional DMs and domain-specific baselines, while vision experiments provide intuitive insights into the limitations of dense DMs and the benefits of SED.

</details>


### [69] [Selector-Guided Autonomous Curriculum for One-Shot Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01823)
*Rudray Dave,Vedang Dubey,Smit Deoghare,Sudhakar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出选择器引导的自主课程学习（SGAC）方法，利用可学习的选择器模型在多维特征空间（成功概率、奖励方差、输出分歧熵、语义难度）中筛选训练实例，代替传统的静态奖励方差启发式方法。实验发现输出分歧（熵）是推理增益的最强预测因子，在MATH基准上使用Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B模型达到68.0%准确率，优于现有最优的66.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于奖励方差启发式的1-shot RLVR实例选择方法存在误导性，无法准确衡量实例的迁移价值，需要更有效的实例选择机制来提升数学推理能力。

Method: 提出Selector-Guided Autonomous Curriculum (SGAC)框架，包含：(1) 构建多维特征空间（成功概率、奖励方差、输出分歧熵、语义难度）；(2) 训练可学习的选择器模型；(3) 基于选择器评分对候选问题池动态排序；(4) 执行微批次1-shot GRPO训练。

Result: 在Hendrycks MATH基准上，使用Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B模型达到68.0%准确率，优于SOTA模型（64.0%）和Wang等人的1-shot RLVR检查点（66.0%）。关键发现：输出分歧熵是比奖励方差更强的推理增益预测因子。

Conclusion: 基于熵的智能数据筛选能在数据严重受限条件下带来严格的推理改进，优于静态训练方法。

Abstract: Recently, Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has been established as a highly effective technique for augmenting the math reasoning skills of Large Language Models (LLMs) based on a single instance. Current state-of-the-art 1-shot RLVR models adopt heuristics for selecting instances, mostly based on historical variance in rewards, which we find to be inherently misleading as a measure of transferability value. In this paper, we propose a Selector-Guided Autonomous Curriculum (SGAC) approach, which employs a learnable selector model on a multi-dimensional feature space consisting of success probability, reward variance, output disagreement (entropy), and semantic difficulty level, instead of the static reward variance heuristic. In our empirical evaluation on pools of candidate problems, we observed that output disagreement, rather than reward variance, is the strongest predictor of reasoning gains in subsequent iterations. Leveraging this finding, we develop an autonomous curriculum algorithm for dynamically siphoning candidate problems from a large pool, ranking them by the learned selector, and running micro-bursts of 1-shot GRPO. Our framework is evaluated using the Hendrycks MATH benchmark, with the Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B model serving as the baseline. Our framework obtains an accuracy of 68.0\% on the hold-out dataset, which is better than the accuracy obtained from the state-of-the-art model, 64.0\%, as well as the 1-shot RLVR checkpoint proposed by Wang et al., which achieved an accuracy of 66.0\%. The results confirm that entropy-based intelligent data curation leads to strict reasoning improvement over static training methods, particularly in severely limited data conditions.

</details>


### [70] [Learning Koopman operators for coupled systems via information on governing equations of subsystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01835)
*Tatsuya Naoi,Jun Ohkubo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用子系统微分方程学习耦合系统Koopman算子的方法，并通过耦合振荡器系统的数值实验验证了其有效性，解决了纯数据驱动方法EDMD在数据有限时不稳定、不准确的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性耦合系统在科学和工程中普遍存在，但由于其高维度和子系统间的复杂相互作用，分析和建模极具挑战性。现有基于Koopman算子的EDMD方法是纯数据驱动的，在数据有限的情况下对耦合系统不稳定且不准确。

Method: 利用控制每个子系统的微分方程来学习耦合系统的Koopman算子，将模型知识融入学习过程，而非纯数据驱动。

Result: 通过在耦合振荡器系统上的数值实验，证明了所提方法在数据有限条件下比纯数据驱动的EDMD方法更稳定、更准确。

Conclusion: 结合子系统微分方程知识学习Koopman算子的方法能有效提升耦合系统建模的稳定性和准确性，为非线性耦合系统的分析提供了新途径。

Abstract: Nonlinear coupled systems are ubiquitous in science and engineering. The analysis and modeling of such systems is challenging due to their high dimensionality and complex interactions among subsystems. In recent years, operator-theoretic methods based on the Koopman operator have attracted attention as a powerful tool for analyzing and modeling nonlinear dynamical systems. Extended dynamic mode decomposition (EDMD) is one of the most popular methods to approximate the Koopman operator. However, EDMD is a purely data-driven method, and it could be unstable and inaccurate for coupled systems under limited data availability. In this paper, we propose a method to learn the Koopman operator for coupled systems using the differential equations governing each subsystem. We also demonstrate its effectiveness through numerical experiments on coupled oscillator systems.

</details>


### [71] [Robust Conditional Conformal Prediction via Branched Normalizing Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01868)
*Rui Xu,Xingyuan Chen,Wenxing Huang,Minxuan Huang,Weiyan Chen,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分支归一化流（BNF）方法，通过可逆传输对齐条件得分分布，在分布偏移下提升条件覆盖鲁棒性，理论证明Wasserstein距离与条件无效性之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有保形预测（CP）方法在分布偏移下仅保证边际覆盖，无法控制单个测试输入的条 件覆盖误差，导致模型在条件得分分布不匹配的区域不可靠。

Method: 提出分支归一化流（BNF），采用双分支架构：一个分支将测试输入归一化到校准分布，另一分支将归一化输入的预测集变换回测试分布，同时保持条件保证。

Result: 在9个数据集上实验，BNF在广泛的置信水平下持续提升了条件覆盖的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 可逆传输（如BNF）能有效缓解分布偏移下保形预测的条件覆盖退化问题。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) constructs prediction sets with marginal coverage guarantees under the assumption that the calibration and test distributions are identical. However, under distribution shift, existing approaches primarily align marginal conformal score distributions, which is sufficient to preserve marginal coverage but does not control the conditional coverage error at individual test inputs. As a consequence, CP can remain unreliable in regions where the conditional score distributions are mismatched. In this work, we bound the conditional invalidity of CP under distribution shift in terms of the Wasserstein distance between the calibration and test distributions. This result highlights the role of invertible transport in mitigating conditional coverage degradation. Motivated by this insight, we introduce Branched Normalizing Flow (BNF), a two-branch architecture that normalizes a test input to the calibration distribution and transforms the prediction set of the normalized input back to the test distribution while preserving conditional guarantees. Empirically, BNF consistently improves conditional coverage robustness on nine datasets across a wide range of confidence levels.

</details>


### [72] [Leveraging Data Symmetries to Select an Optimal Subset of Training Data under Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01874)
*Kumar Shubham,Pavan Karjol,Kiran M K,Prathosh AP*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在标签噪声环境下，通过cutstats方法（基于k-NN）识别低噪声子集的有效性，证明k-NN的准确性直接影响最终分类器性能，并提出利用数据不变性和对称性知识可显著提升高维场景下的k-NN性能，即使仅部分已知不变性，学习到的不变表示仍有助于找到近似最优子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有cutstats方法在高维数据上的性能尚未被充分探索，且缺乏理论分析来阐明其有效性受何种因素影响。

Method: 首先从理论上形式化建立了cutstats选择的子集训练的分类器性能与k-NN准确率之间的关系；然后提出在噪声环境下利用数据不变性和对称性知识来增强k-NN性能；最后在真实场景中利用学习到的不变表示来近似实现这一改进。

Result: 理论上证明了k-NN准确率是影响cutstats子集分类性能的关键因素；实验表明利用不变性可使k-NN在高维噪声环境下接近贝叶斯最优分类器；在仅部分已知不变性的真实场景中，学习到的不变表示仍能帮助识别近乎最优的子集。

Conclusion: 利用数据不变性和对称性可以显著提升cutstats在高维标签噪声环境下的子集选择效果，即使不变性信息不完整也能获得近似最优结果。

Abstract: The performance of machine learning models often relies on large labeled datasets; however, data collected from diverse sources can contain label noise. Recent work has shown that, in noisy settings, there may exist a subset of the training data on which models can achieve performance comparable to training on a noise-free dataset. A widely used method for identifying such subsets is cutstats, which employs k-nearest neighbors (k-NN) to detect low-noise samples. However, its performance on high-dimensional data remains largely unexplored. In this work, we formally establish that the performance of a classifier trained on a subset of a noisy dataset selected via cutstats is influenced by the accuracy of k-NN. We further demonstrate that, in noisy environments, exploiting data invariance and knowledge of underlying symmetries can significantly enhance the performance of k-NN, bringing it closer to the Bayes optimal classifier even in high-dimensional regimes. Finally, we show that for real-world scenarios, where information about the underlying invariance is only partially known, learnt invariant representations can still facilitate the identification of near-optimal subsets.

</details>


### [73] [How Label Imbalance Shapes Geometry: A General Spectral Analysis of Multi-Label Neural Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01897)
*Xiaoxuan Ma,Yixuan Yang,Song Li,Xiangyun Hui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多标签分类中的神经坍缩现象，提出了谱控制框架来刻画标签不平衡和相关性导致的几何失真，证明了标签协方差谱控制终端相位的稳定性，并解决了多重性不平衡猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将神经坍缩的概念从多类学习扩展到多标签分类，解决现有"标签平均化"视角无法解释标签相关性和数据不平衡导致的几何失真的问题。

Method: 提出谱控制框架，引入标签协方差谱κ_m（标签分布二阶矩矩阵的标量控制量），分析终端相位下的几何结构，证明高阶多重性原型遵循类频率加权合成规则而非均匀平均。

Result: 证明了标签协方差谱控制终端几何的稳定性，量化了最弱中心化类间对比方向；经典标签平均化结构仅在完全正交条件下成立；数值实验验证了理论边界。

Conclusion: 解决了不平衡猜想中的缩放平均问题，建立了将神经坍缩推广到复杂不平衡多标签场景的统一理论框架。

Abstract: This work investigates the phenomenon of Neural Collapse (NC) in multi-label classification, extending its conceptual framework from multi-class learning to general correlated and imbalanced multi-label settings. Although recent studies have identified a ''tag-wise averaging'' structure for multi-label features, this view relies on implicit assumptions of label balance and combinatorial symmetry. Consequently, it fails to account for the geometrical distortions caused by intrinsic label correlations and data imbalance, which are common in practice. We resolve the multiplicity-one imbalance conjecture raised by Li et al. (2024), showing that higher-multiplicity prototypes obey a class-frequency-weighted synthesis rule rather than uniform averaging. To address this, we propose a rigorous spectral-control framework to analyze the terminal phase of multi-label learning under general imbalanced conditions. We introduce the label covariance spectrum $κ_m$, a scalar controlling the distribution-dependent lower-bound geometry, derived from the second-order moment matrix of the label distribution. Contrary to the averaging perspective, our analysis reveals that the centered label covariance spectrum controls the stability of terminal geometry by quantifying the weakest centered inter-class contrast directions. We prove that the classical Tag-wise Averaging emerges only as a special case under perfect orthogonality. Numerical experiments on synthetic distributions validate our theoretical bounds. This work resolves the scaled-average aspect of the imbalance conjecture and establishes a unifying theoretical framework that extends Neural Collapse to complex, imbalanced multi-label settings.

</details>


### [74] [Stochastic Sparse Attention for Memory-Bound Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01910)
*Kyle Lee,Corentin Delacour,Kevin Callahan-Coray,Kyle Jiang,Can Yaras,Samet Oymak,Tathagata Srimani,Kerem Y. Camsari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SANTA提出了一种随机加法无乘法注意力机制，通过后softmax分布采样稀疏化值缓存访问，并用gather-add替代乘加运算，在32k上下文下实现1.5倍解码注意力核加速，同时提出伯努利qK^T采样进一步稀疏化分数阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文自回归解码中，每个token生成需要读取KV缓存中所有nk个键值向量，导致带宽瓶颈。

Method: 1) 从后softmax分布中采样S≪nk个索引，仅聚合这些值行，得到无偏估计器；2) 引入分层采样设计方差缩减的GPU友好变体；3) 提出伯努利qK^T采样作为互补技术，通过随机三元查询稀疏化分数阶段。

Result: 在NVIDIA RTX 6000 Ada上相比FlashInfer和FlashDecoding实现1.5倍解码注意力核加速，在32k上下文下匹配基线精度。

Conclusion: SANTA与三元量化、低秩投影、KV缓存压缩等上游技术正交，共同指向稀疏、无乘法器、节能的推理方向。

Abstract: Autoregressive decoding becomes bandwidth-limited at long contexts, as generating each token requires reading all $n_k$ key and value vectors from KV cache. We present Stochastic Additive No-mulT Attention (SANTA), a method that sparsifies value-cache access by sampling $S \ll n_k$ indices from the post-softmax distribution and aggregates only those value rows. This yields an unbiased estimator of the post-softmax value aggregation while replacing value-stage multiply-accumulates with gather-and-add. We introduce stratified sampling to design variance-reduced, GPU-friendly variants, demonstrating $1.5\times$ decode-step attention kernel speedup over FlashInfer and FlashDecoding on an NVIDIA RTX 6000 Ada while matching baseline accuracy at 32k-token contexts. Finally, we propose Bernoulli $qK^\mathsf{T}$ sampling as a complementary technique to sparsify the score stage, reducing key-feature access through stochastic ternary queries. Both methods are orthogonal to upstream techniques such as ternary quantization, low-rank projections, and KV-cache compression. Together, they point toward sparse, multiplier-free, and energy-efficient inference. We open-source our kernels at: https://github.com/OPUSLab/SANTA.git

</details>


### [75] [RefusalGuard: Geometry-Preserving Fine-Tuning for Safety in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01913)
*Sadia Asif,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了安全对齐语言模型在微调过程中安全表示的结构性退化机制，并提出REFUSALGUARD框架，通过在表示空间约束更新来保持安全相关结构的稳定性，在多个模型和基准上实现了与原始安全对齐模型相当的攻击成功率，同时保持了良好的下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调安全对齐语言模型用于下游任务时，常导致拒绝行为大幅退化，使模型易受对抗性滥用。尽管已有工作表明安全相关特征在模型激活空间中以结构化表示形式编码，但这些表示在微调过程中如何变化以及对齐为何退化仍缺乏理解。

Method: 通过分析微调过程中安全相关表示的系统性漂移、几何结构扭曲以及任务优化与安全特征之间的干扰，揭示对齐退化机制；并提出REFUSALGUARD，一种表示级微调框架，通过在隐藏表示空间中约束更新，在保持安全中介组件稳定的同时允许任务特定学习在互补方向上进行。

Result: 在LLaMA、Gemma、Qwen等多个模型家族上，针对AdvBench、DirectHarm4、JailbreakBench等对抗性安全基准以及下游任务进行评估，REFUSALGUARD实现了与基础安全对齐模型相当的攻击成功率，同时保持了有竞争力的任务性能，显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 微调导致的安全对齐退化源于表示层面的系统性漂移和结构扭曲，通过表示空间的约束更新可以有效缓解这一问题，实现安全性与任务性能的兼顾。

Abstract: Fine-tuning safety-aligned language models for downstream tasks often leads to substantial degradation of refusal behavior, making models vulnerable to adversarial misuse. While prior work has shown that safety-relevant features are encoded in structured representations within the model's activation space, how these representations change during fine-tuning and why alignment degrades remains poorly understood. In this work, we investigate the representation-level mechanisms underlying alignment degradation. Our analysis shows that standard fine-tuning induces systematic drift in safety-relevant representations, distorts their geometric structure, and introduces interference between task optimization and safety features. These effects collectively lead to increased harmful compliance. Motivated by these findings, we introduce REFUSALGUARD, a representation-level fine-tuning framework that preserves safety-relevant structure during model adaptation. Our approach constrains updates in hidden representation space, ensuring that safety-mediating components remain stable while allowing task-specific learning in complementary directions. We evaluate REFUSALGUARD across multiple model families, including LLaMA, Gemma, and Qwen, on adversarial safety benchmarks such as AdvBench, DirectHarm4, and JailbreakBench, as well as downstream utility tasks. Our approach achieves attack success rates comparable to base safety-aligned models while maintaining competitive task performance, significantly outperforming baselines.

</details>


### [76] [Pandora's Regret: A Proper Scoring Rule for Evaluating Sequential Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01936)
*Gerardo A. Flores,Yash Deshpande,Jannis R. Brea,Ashia C. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潘多拉遗憾（Pandora's Regret），一种基于序贯搜索期望成本的成对可加严格适当评分规则，可同时校准概率并惩罚排名颠倒错误，在医疗诊断等场景优于传统指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准评分规则（如对数损失）是局部的，忽略了候选项的排序，与序贯搜索的实际效用不一致。需要一种能同时反映概率校准与排序质量的评分规则。

Method: 通过分析不同测试成本下最优序贯搜索的期望成本，推导出成对可加且严格适当的评分规则——潘多拉遗憾。构造了一个单参数Beta族来平衡排名交换惩罚与概率幅度的权衡。

Result: 证明了对数损失、准确率和宏F1依赖于与序贯搜索不一致的隐式决策模型。在597个MedMNIST模型上，潘多拉指标比标准替代指标更好地预测临床诊断成本。

Conclusion: 将决策理论评分规则构建扩展到多类设置，提出了一个既有概率校准又有排序感知的评分规则，在多类分类评估中具有理论和实践价值。

Abstract: In sequential search, alternatives are tested until the true class is found. Standard proper scoring rules like log loss are local, ignoring the ranking of competitors and misaligning model evaluation with search utility. We show that sequential search induces a pairwise structure that overcomes this. By analyzing the expected cost of optimal search under varying testing costs, we derive Pandora's Regret: a closed-form, pairwise-additive, and strictly proper scoring rule. Pandora's Regret both elicits true probabilities and penalizes rank-reversing miscalibrations where distractors outrank the true class. Our construction yields a one-parameter Beta family that balances penalties for rank-swapping versus probability magnitude, while retaining a grounded interpretation as expected search cost. We prove that log loss, accuracy, and macro-F1 rely on implicit decision models misaligned with sequential search. Across 597 MedMNIST models, Pandora-based metrics better predict clinical diagnostic costs than standard alternatives, extending decision-theoretic scoring rule construction to the multiclass setting.

</details>


### [77] [PepSpecBench: A Unified Evaluation Benchmark for Peptide Tandem Mass Spectrometry Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01945)
*Zhiwen Yang,Pan Liu,Yifan Li,Yunhua Zhong,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PepSpecBench，一个统一基准框架，用于解决肽段MS/MS谱图预测中数据预处理不一致、数据划分泄露序列信息、缺乏跨物种评估与鲁棒性测试三大挑战，通过标准化流程、严格的主干分离策略和多物种评估套件，揭示了现有六种代表性模型的性能差异与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前肽段MS/MS谱图预测领域存在三大评估挑战：（1）数据预处理不一致和模型输出空间不兼容导致公平比较困难；（2）有缺陷的数据划分策略可能造成隐藏的序列泄露，夸大性能；（3）缺乏跨物种基准评估和针对关键实验条件的系统鲁棒性分析，使领域真实进展被遮蔽。

Method: 构建PepSpecBench基准：（1）标准化多个公共数据集的预处理流程；（2）采用严格的主干分离（backbone-disjoint）数据划分策略消除序列泄露；（3）在统一的碎片离子表示空间中评估多种架构；（4）引入多物种评估套件和基于物理信息的元数据扰动探针，评估模型鲁棒性与仪器感知能力。

Result: 在六种代表性模型上揭示了此前未被识别的性能差异和鲁棒性限制，为未来模型设计、评估和实际部署提供了可操作的建议。

Conclusion: PepSpecBench通过统一基准框架有效解决了现有评估中的关键问题，证明了标准化评估对准确衡量领域进展的重要性，并为质谱蛋白质组学中谱图预测模型的公平比较和可靠性评估奠定了基础。

Abstract: Tandem mass spectrometry provides a high-throughput framework for identifying and quantifying proteins in complex biological samples. In computational proteomics, predicting peptide MS/MS spectra is a critical task, enabling downstream applications such as large-scale peptide identification and quantification. While deep learning architectures have substantially improved prediction accuracy, three evaluation challenges obscure the true progress of the field. First, inconsistent data preprocessing and incompatible model output spaces hinder fair model comparison. Second, flawed data splitting strategies can permit hidden sequence leakage and inflate reported performance. Third, existing evaluations typically lack comprehensive cross-species benchmarking and systematic assessment of model robustness to influential experimental conditions. To address these challenges, we propose PepSpecBench, a unified benchmark for peptide MS/MS spectrum prediction. PepSpecBench standardizes data preprocessing across complementary public datasets, enforces a strict backbone-disjoint splitting strategy to eliminate sequence leakage, and evaluates diverse architectures within a shared fragment-ion representation space. It further introduces a comprehensive multi-species evaluation suite and physically grounded metadata perturbation probes to assess model robustness and instrument awareness. We uncover previously unrecognized performance discrepancies and robustness limitations across six representative models, providing actionable insights for future model design, evaluation and practical deployment.

</details>


### [78] [TRAP: Tail-aware Ranking Attack for World-Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01950)
*Siyuan Duan,Ke Zhang,Xizhao Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出针对世界模型的后门攻击框架TRAP，通过操纵想象轨迹的排序来劫持规划过程，揭示了世界模型在长尾排序结构上的独特脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击主要针对局部特征或单步预测，对具有动力学先验和规划过程的世界模型效果有限，因为世界模型能吸收或冲刷浅层扰动。作者发现世界模型存在一种基于想象轨迹长尾排序结构的独特后门脆弱性。

Method: 提出TRAP框架，包含：(1) 尾部感知排序损失，聚焦优化决策关键轨迹；(2) 双重门控机制，稳定优化并控制攻击惩罚的施加时机和位置。在触发条件下改变关键轨迹的相对排序以重定向规划结果，同时在干净输入上保持正常排序结构。

Result: 在DreamerV3和TD-MPC2上跨多个任务实验，TRAP持续诱导行为偏差并造成显著的性能下降。

Conclusion: 世界模型存在基于想象轨迹排序的新型后门漏洞，需要针对基于世界模型的智能体进行专门的安全评估。

Abstract: World models enable long-horizon planning by internally generating and evaluating imagined trajectories, making them a promising foundation for generalist agents. However, this imagination-driven decision process also introduces new security risks. Existing backdoor attacks typically aim to manipulate local features, one-step predictions, or instantaneous policy outputs. While such objectives may suffice for weaker reactive models, they are often ineffective against world models, where the learned dynamics prior and planning process can absorb or wash out the effects of shallow perturbations. More importantly, we find that world models exhibit a distinct backdoor vulnerability rooted in the long-tailed ranking structure of imagined trajectories, where disrupting the ordering of a few decision-critical trajectories can systematically hijack planning.
  To exploit this vulnerability, we propose TRAP, a backdoor attack framework for world models that targets imagined trajectory ranking. TRAP combines a tail-aware ranking loss to focus optimization on decision-critical trajectories with dual gating mechanisms that stabilize optimization and regulate when and where the attack penalty is applied. Under trigger conditions, TRAP alters the relative ranking of imagined trajectories to redirect planning outcomes, while largely maintaining the normal ranking structure on clean inputs. Experiments on DreamerV3 and TD-MPC2 across diverse tasks show that TRAP consistently induces sustained behavioral deviations and significant performance degradation, highlighting the need for dedicated security evaluation of world-model-based agents.

</details>


### [79] [Flexi-LoRA with Input-Adaptive Ranks: Efficient Finetuning for Speech and Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01959)
*Zongqian Li,Yixuan Su,Han Zhou,Zihao Fu,Nigel Collier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flexi-LoRA提出了一种动态调整LoRA秩的方法，根据输入复杂度在训练和推理时灵活分配参数，在减少参数量的同时提升了模型性能，尤其是在数学推理等需要严格推理链的任务中效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LoRA方法采用静态参数分配，无法适应不同输入复杂度的变化，导致参数利用效率低下。作者希望实现一种输入自适应的参数分配机制，以更少的参数获得更优的性能。

Method: 提出Flexi-LoRA框架，根据输入复杂度动态调整LoRA的秩（rank），在训练和推理阶段保持秩动态的一致性。该方法通过输入依赖的参数分配策略，将秩配置与问题复杂度最优匹配。

Result: 在问答、数学推理和语音任务上，Flexi-LoRA以更少的参数持续优于静态LoRA；数学推理任务对秩动态的依赖性高于QA任务；性能提升在需要严格推理链的任务上更为显著。

Conclusion: 输入自适应的参数分配方法可以有效减少参数冗余并提升模型能力，以更简洁的实现方式实现了类似混合专家（MoE）框架的核心优势，为未来输入自适应高效微调方法奠定了基础。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods like Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) have become essential for deploying large language models, yet their static parameter allocation remains suboptimal for inputs of varying complexity. We present Flexi-LoRA, a novel framework that dynamically adjusts LoRA ranks based on input complexity during both training and inference. Through empirical analysis across question answering, mathematical reasoning, and speech tasks, we demonstrate that maintaining consistency between training and inference dynamics is important for effective adaptation, particularly for sequential reasoning tasks. Our findings reveal that input-dependent parameter allocation achieves higher performance with fewer parameters by optimally matching rank configurations to question complexity. Furthermore, task-specific dependency on rank dynamics varies, with mathematical reasoning tasks exhibiting higher dependency than QA tasks. Successful adaptation manifests not only in correctness but also in reasoning quality and instruction adherence. Flexi-LoRA consistently outperforms static LoRA while using fewer parameters, with performance gains more pronounced on tasks requiring strict reasoning chains. Our approach realizes key benefits of mixture-of-experts frameworks through a more streamlined implementation, reducing parameter redundancy while improving model capabilities. We provide comprehensive empirical studies across diverse tasks, establishing a basis for future work in input-adaptive and efficient fine-tuning approaches.

</details>


### [80] [Multi-User Dueling Bandits: A Fair Approach using Nash Social Welfare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01961)
*Maheed H. Ahmed,Mahsa Ghasemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线偏好学习（dueling bandits）中的公平性问题。假设每个用户有各自的Condorcet胜者，采用Nash社会福利目标最大化用户效用乘积，首次推导出公平性带来的遗憾下界Ω(T^{2/3} min(K,D)^{1/3})，并提出Fair-Explore-Then-Commit和Fair-ε-Greedy算法，其遗憾上界与下界在T上匹配至对数因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好学习模型通常基于所有人类评估者的平均偏好训练模型，当偏好存在较大差异时，这种平均化处理可能对少数群体不公平。本文旨在heterogeneous偏好场景下引入公平性约束。

Method: 以每个用户各自的Condorcet胜者为参考点，采用Nash社会福利目标（最大化用户效用乘积）作为公平性度量框架。构建困难实例推导遗憾下界，提出Fair-Explore-Then-Commit和Fair-ε-Greedy两种算法（含Condorcet胜者识别阶段）。

Result: 首次量化heterogeneous偏好下dueling bandits中公平性的代价：遗憾下界Ω(T^{2/3} min(K,D)^{1/3})；所提算法的遗憾上界在T的阶上与下界匹配（至多相差对数因子）。

Conclusion: 在dueling bandits框架下通过Nash社会福利目标可以实现公平性，且公平性带来的额外代价（遗憾）存在理论下界，所提算法达到近乎最优。

Abstract: Learning from human preference data is becoming a useful tool, from fine-tuning large language models to training reinforcement learning agents. However, in most scenarios, the model is trained on the average preference of all human evaluators, which, under large variations of preferences, can be unfair to minority groups. In this work, we consider fairness in dueling bandits, a standard framework for online learning from preference data. We assume that each user has a (potentially distinct) Condorcet winner, which is an arm preferred to every other arm. Using these user-specific Condorcet winners as reference points, we evaluate and score arms according to their performance relative to the corresponding winner. To promote fairness across heterogeneous users, we adopt the well-established Nash Social Welfare objective, which maximizes the product of user utilities, thereby inherently penalizing inequality and preventing the marginalization of any single user. Within this framework, we construct a hard instance to establish a regret lower bound of $Ω(T^{2/3}\min(K,D)^\frac{1}{3})$ for a time horizon $T$, $K$ arms, and $D$ users, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first result quantifying the cost of fairness in dueling bandits with heterogeneous preferences. We then present the Fair-Explore-Then-Commit and Fair-$ε$-Greedy algorithms with a Condorcet winner identification phase. We further derive their regret upper bounds that match the lower-bound dependence on $T$ up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [81] [Retrieval with Multiple Query Vectors through Anomalous Pattern Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01965)
*Allassan Tchangmena A Nken,Baimam Boukar Jean Jacques,Miriam Rateike,Celia Cintas,Skyler Speakman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用异常模式检测的多查询向量检索方法，通过识别查询向量集中的异常维度，并检索数据库中在这些维度上也呈现异常的向量。实验表明，增加查询集大小（从1到8）能显著提升检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统向量检索仅考虑单个查询向量，但复杂推理和检索任务往往需要多个查询向量同时输入。现有方法无法有效利用多个查询向量中的协同信息。

Method: 输入多个查询向量集合Q（|Q|≥1），识别Q中哪些维度呈现异常（突出于其他维度），然后在向量数据库中扫描并检索在这些异常维度上同样呈现异常的向量。

Result: 在图像、文本和表格数据集上验证，更大的查询集通常带来更好的检索性能。从1个查询增加到8个时提升最显著，继续增加后收益递减。

Conclusion: 多查询向量检索方法能有效提升检索性能，尤其适合需要复杂推理的任务场景。

Abstract: A classical vector retrieval problem typically considers a \emph{single} query embedding vector as input and retrieves the most similar embedding vectors from a vector database. However, complex reasoning and retrieval tasks frequently require \emph{multiple query vectors}, rather than a single one. In this work, we propose a retrieval method that considers multiple query vectors simultaneously and retrieves the most relevant vectors from the database using concepts from anomalous pattern detection. Specifically, our approach leverages a set of query vectors $Q$ (with $|Q|\geq 1$), and identifies the subset of vector dimensions within $Q$ that standout (anomalous) from the rest of dimensions. Next, we scan the vector database to retrieve the set of vectors that are also anomalous across the previously identified vector dimensions and return them as our retrieved set of vectors. We validate our approach on two image datasets, a text dataset, and a tabular dataset. Overall, we observe that, across most datasets, larger query sets lead to improved retrieval performance. The improvement is most pronounced when increasing the query sets from 1 to 8, while the gains become smaller beyond that.

</details>


### [82] [MER-DG: Modality-Entropy Regularization for Multimodal Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01967)
*Yavuz Yarici,Ghassan AlRegib*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为MER-DG的正则化方法，通过最大化每个编码器特征分布的熵来防止多模态模型中的融合过拟合，在域泛化任务上取得约5%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态域泛化方法中，联合优化导致各模态编码器利用源域特定记录条件下的跨模态共现关系（即模态间的统计关联），而非学习域不变特征，这种现象被称为融合过拟合（Fusion Overfitting）。

Method: 提出Modality-Entropy Regularization for Domain Generalization (MER-DG)，通过最大化每个编码器特征分布的熵来保留特征多样性，作为附加损失项可与现有任意多模态框架集成。

Result: 在EPIC-Kitchens和HAC基准上，相比标准融合方法平均提升约5%，相比现有最优方法平均提升约2%。

Conclusion: MER-DG是一种架构无关的有效正则化方法，能够缓解多模态域泛化中的融合过拟合问题，提升模型在域外场景的泛化能力。

Abstract: Deploying multimodal models in real-world scenarios requires generalization to new environments where recording conditions differ from training, a challenge known as multimodal domain generalization (MMDG). Standard architectures employ separate encoders for each modality and a fusion module, training the system end-to-end by optimizing on the fused features. In this paper, we identify that such joint optimization causes encoders to exploit cross-modal co-occurrences, statistical relationships between modalities that arise from source-specific recording conditions, rather than learning domain-invariant features. We term this failure mode Fusion Overfitting. To address this, we propose Modality-Entropy Regularization for Domain Generalization (MER-DG), which maximizes the entropy of each encoder's feature distribution to preserve feature diversity. MER-DG is architecture-agnostic and integrates into existing multimodal frameworks as an additive loss term. Extensive experiments on EPIC-Kitchens and HAC benchmarks demonstrate average improvements of approximately 5% over standard fusion and approximately 2% over state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [83] [RamanBench: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Machine Learning on Raman Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02003)
*Mario Koddenbrock,Christoph Lange,Robin Legner,Martin Jäger,Martin Kögler,Mariano N. Cruz Bournazou,Peter Neubauer,Felix Biessmann,Erik Rodner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RamanBench 是首个大规模、完全可复现的拉曼光谱机器学习基准，包含 74 个数据集、325,668 条光谱，统一了评估协议并发布了排行榜。基准测试发现表格基础模型（TFM）表现最佳，但没有任何方法能跨数据集泛化，揭示了该领域的根本性缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉曼光谱领域的机器学习研究受限于数据集碎片化、评估不一致以及模型未能捕捉光谱数据结构，缺乏标准化基准。

Method: 整合 74 个数据集（含 16 个新发布），涵盖分类与回归任务，统一数据访问、评估协议和代码，建立在线排行榜。在标准化协议下基准测试 28 个模型，包括经典方法、拉曼专用模型、表格基础模型和时间序列方法。

Result: 表格基础模型（如 TabPFN）一致优于领域专用模型和梯度提升基线；时间序列模型（如 ROCKET）具有竞争力；但没有任何方法能跨数据集泛化。

Conclusion: 现有方法在拉曼光谱上缺乏跨数据集泛化能力，需要社区贡献新方法，以推动医学诊断、生物学研究和材料科学等关键应用的发展。

Abstract: Machine Learning (ML) has transformed many scientific fields, yet key applications still lack standardized benchmarks. Raman spectroscopy, a widely used technique for non-invasive molecular analysis, is one such field where progress is limited by fragmented datasets, inconsistent evaluation, and models that fail to capture the structure of spectral data. We introduce RamanBench, the first large-scale, fully reproducible benchmark for ML on Raman spectroscopy, consisting of streamlined data access, evaluation protocols and code, as well as a live leaderboard. It unifies 74 datasets (including 16 first released with this benchmark) across four domains, comprising 325,668 spectra and spanning classification and regression tasks under diverse experimental conditions. We benchmark 28 models under a standardized protocol, including classical methods (e.g., PLS), Raman-specific (e.g., RamanNet), Tabular Foundation Model (TFM) (e.g., TabPFN), and time-series approaches (e.g., ROCKET). TFM consistently outperform domain-specific and gradient boosting baselines, while time-series models remain competitive. However, no method generalizes across datasets, revealing a fundamental gap. Therefore, we invite the community to contribute new approaches to our living benchmark, with the potential to accelerate advances in critical applications such as medical diagnostics, biological research, and materials science.

</details>


### [84] [How Can One Choose the Best CAM-Based Explainability Method for a CNN Model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02007)
*Daniel da Silva Costa,Pedro Nuno de Souza Moura,Adriana C. F. Alvim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用多种距离度量（曼哈顿距离和相关性）来评估可解释性方法生成的显著性图与人类感知的相似性，通过众包实验发现LayerCAM、Score-CAM和IS-CAM最符合人类感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法缺乏有效的评估指标，常用的IoU指标因显著性图无固定形状而不够理想，需要找到更能反映人类感知的评估指标。

Method: 使用ImageNet中Chihuahua子集，采用多种CAM-based可解释性方法生成显著性图，通过距离度量计算与人工标注边界框的对齐程度，利用众包收集人类偏好排序，使用RBO指标比较不同度量的排序与人类排序的一致性。

Result: 曼哈顿距离和相关性两种度量最接近人类感知；LayerCAM、Score-CAM和IS-CAM是最符合人类感知的可解释性方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法框架能够有效找到与人类感知最一致的可解释性方法及评估指标。

Abstract: In recent years, several advances have been observed in Deep Learning with surprising results. Models in this area have been increasingly used in numerous applications, including those sensitive to human life, which require clear explanations and justifications. Various explainability methods have been proposed, but not many metrics to evaluate these methods. The most commonly used metric is the Intersection over Union (IoU). However, due to the characteristics of the results of the explainability methods, called saliency maps, which do not have a known shape, we hypothesise that there must be a better metric that allows one to find an explainability method that produces results that best resemble the human perception. We propose using different metrics to assess the similarity between human perception and the explanation saliency maps to find a better metric. An investigation was conducted employing a subset of the Chihuahuas images from ImageNet dataset. Several CAM-based explainability methods were used to generate saliency maps for each chihuahua image. Alignment was measured by applying distance metrics between the bounding box of human annotations and the saliency maps produced by each explainability method. Rankings of the best saliency maps were created using the results of the distance metrics and compared to the ranking obtained using people's choice, collected through crowdsourcing, of the best explanation saliency maps for each selected image. Comparison between rankings was performed using the Rank-Biased Overlap (RBO) metric. The results indicate the feasibility of our method to find the explainability method that best resembles human perception. In our experiments, the two metrics that best resemble human perception corresponded to Manhattan and Correlation. Besides, the best explainability methods regarding human perception were LayerCAM, Score-CAM, and IS-CAM.

</details>


### [85] [Large margin classifier with graph-based adaptive regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02027)
*Vítor M. Hanriot,Turíbio T. Salis,Luiz C. B. Torres,Frederico Coelho,Antonio P. Braga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在基于Gabriel图的二分类器中引入每类正则化超参数，通过灵活调整分类阈值来消除异常值并处理类别不平衡问题，Friedman检验表明该方法能有效提升分类器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Gabriel图分类器使用固定阈值，在存在异常值和类别不平衡时性能受限。本文旨在通过引入每类可调的正则化超参数来扩展解空间，从而同时解决异常值消除和类别不平衡问题。

Method: 在Gabriel图分类器中为每个类别分别引入正则化超参数；利用质量索引在边界区域和异常值存在时的行为特性进行正则化；通过超参数调优算法优化灵活阈值。

Result: 灵活阈值方案相比固定阈值方案扩展了解空间；能够有效消除异常值；能为多数类和少数类分别生成更高和更低的阈值以应对类别不平衡；Friedman检验证实灵活阈值能显著提升Gabriel图分类器性能。

Conclusion: 每类正则化超参数的引入是改进Gabriel图二分类器的一种有效策略，能够同时处理异常值和类别不平衡两大挑战。

Abstract: This paper introduces the use of per-class regularization hyperparameters in Gabriel graph-based binary classifiers. We demonstrate how the quality index used for regularization behaves both in the margin region and in the presence of outliers, and how incorporating this regularization flexibility can lead to solutions that effectively eliminate outliers while training the classifier. We also show how it can address class imbalance by generating higher and lower thresholds for the majority and minority classes, respectively. Thus, rather than having a single solution based on fixed thresholds, flexible thresholds expand the solution space and can be optimized through hyperparameter tuning algorithms. Friedman test shows that flexible thresholds are capable of improving Gabriel graph-based classifiers.

</details>


### [86] [Bringing Order to Asynchronous SGD: Towards Optimality under Data-Dependent Delays with Momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02043)
*Tehila Dahan,Roie Reshef,Sharon Goldstein,Kfir Y. Levy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于动量的异步SGD框架，能够在数据依赖延迟场景下保留延迟梯度信息，并首次在凸和非凸光滑设定下实现了最优收敛速率，同时推导了鲁棒的学习率调度方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异步SGD方法通过衰减或丢弃延迟梯度来缓解梯度陈旧问题，但引入了系统性偏差——简单或快速处理的样本过表示，复杂样本的梯度被延迟或抑制。此前数据依赖延迟的方法依赖于Lipschitz假设导致次优速率，且未处理光滑凸情形。

Method: 提出基于动量的异步优化框架，在保留延迟梯度信息的同时减轻陈旧性影响，并推导了鲁棒的学习率调度方案。

Result: 首次在数据依赖延迟下，针对光滑凸和光滑非凸设定均建立了最优收敛速率。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了异步SGD中延迟梯度带来的偏差问题，简化了实际中的超参数调优。

Abstract: Asynchronous stochastic gradient descent (SGD) enables scalable distributed training but suffers from gradient staleness. Existing mitigation strategies, such as delay-adaptive learning rates and staleness-aware filtering, typically attenuate or discard delayed gradients, introducing systematic bias: updates from simpler or faster-to-process samples are overrepresented, while gradients from more complex samples are delayed or suppressed. In contrast, prior approaches to data-dependent delays rely on a Lipschitz assumption that yields suboptimal rates or leave the smooth, convex case unaddressed. We propose a momentum-based asynchronous framework designed to preserve information from delayed gradients while mitigating the effects of staleness. We establish the first optimal convergence rates for data-dependent delays in both convex and non-convex smooth setups, providing a new result for asynchronous optimization under standard assumptions. Additionally, we derive robust learning-rate schedules that simplify hyperparameter tuning in practice.

</details>


### [87] [DR-SNE: Density-Regularized Stochastic Neighbor Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02060)
*Maksim Kazanskii*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DR-SNE通过将密度正则化项引入SNE目标函数，实现了条件结构与相对密度结构的联合对齐，相比现有方法在保持邻域结构的同时更好地保留了数据密度信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: t-SNE等降维方法仅关注局部邻域结构保持，未显式考虑概率质量分布，导致数据密度失真。作者希望将密度信息纳入降维过程，实现几何结构与密度结构的联合优化。

Method: 将降维重构为条件结构（局部关系）与相对密度结构（局部密度统计）的联合对齐，提出DR-SNE，在SNE目标函数中引入基于归一化对数密度估计的密度正则化项，实现尺度不变的密度相对变化保持。

Result: DR-SNE在保持邻域保真度的同时显著提升了密度保持能力，在异常检测等密度敏感任务中跨多个数据集取得性能提升。

Conclusion: 在降维中融入密度信息可以有效补充纯几何导向的目标函数，密度正则化是一种简单且有效的密度保持机制。

Abstract: Dimensionality reduction methods such as t-SNE are designed to preserve local neighborhood structure but do not explicitly account for how probability mass is distributed, often leading to distortions of data density. We reformulate dimensionality reduction as the joint alignment of two components: (i) conditional structure, capturing local relationships, and (ii) relative density structure, captured via local density statistics. Based on this perspective, we introduce Density-Regularized SNE (DR-SNE), which augments the stochastic neighbor embedding objective with a density regularization term derived from normalized log-density estimates. Unlike prior approaches such as DensMAP and DenSNE, which rely on local scale consistency, DR-SNE directly aligns normalized density estimates, providing a simple and scale-invariant mechanism for preserving relative density variations. Empirically, DR-SNE improves density preservation while maintaining competitive neighborhood fidelity, and yields gains on density-sensitive tasks such as anomaly detection across multiple datasets. These results suggest that incorporating density information complements geometry-focused objectives in dimensionality reduction.

</details>


### [88] [Weight Clipping for Robust Conformal Inference under Unbounded Covariate Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02072)
*James Wang,Surbhi Goel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对加权共形预测（WCP）在协变量偏移下因密度比无界或估计不准导致的覆盖不足问题，提出了裁剪最小二乘重要性拟合（CLISF）方法进行低方差密度比估计，并理论证明了其有界期望覆盖不足，且可通过略微膨胀覆盖目标进行校正，提供了首个关于权重裁剪的共形推理理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测的交换性假设在实践中常因协变量偏移被违反，现有加权共形预测（WCP）在密度比无界或需学习时存在严重的覆盖不足问题，原因是密度比学习的过拟合以及非一致性得分阈值估计的高方差。

Method: 提出裁剪最小二乘重要性拟合（CLISF）作为降方差密度比估计方法；证明使用CLISF学习密度比并接入WCP时，期望覆盖不足有界；进一步证明可通过略微膨胀覆盖目标来校正覆盖不足，并从数据中估计所需膨胀水平。

Result: 提供了共形推理中权重裁剪的首个理论保证，实现了数据集条件覆盖，样本复杂度不随真实密度比的高阶矩爆炸（克服了先前工作的关键局限）。在真实基准和合成数据上验证了结果。

Conclusion: CLISF有效解决了WCP在协变量偏移下的覆盖不足问题，理论上有界且可校正，为实际应用提供了更可靠的分布自由预测集构建方法。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) provides powerful, distribution-free prediction sets, but its guarantees rely on the exchangeability of training and test data, which is often violated in practice due to covariate shifts. While weighted conformal prediction (WCP) is designed to handle such shifts, it can suffer from significant undercoverage when the density ratio between the distributions is unbounded and/or must be learned. This is because of both overfitting in learning the density ratio, and high variance in estimating the nonconformity score threshold. To address this, we introduce clipped least-squares importance fitting (CLISF) as a reduced-variance method for density ratio estimation. Specifically, we show that density ratios learned using CLISF, when plugged into WCP, have bounded expected undercoverage. Furthermore, we show that the undercoverage can be corrected by running WCP with a slightly inflated coverage target; crucially, we are able to estimate the required level of inflation from the data. We provide the first theoretical guarantees for weight clipping in conformal inference, achieving dataset-conditional coverage with a sample complexity that does not blow up with the higher moments of the true density ratio -- a key limitation of prior work. We verify our results on real-world benchmarks and synthetic data.

</details>


### [89] [GETA-3DGS: Automatic Joint Structured Pruning and Quantization for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02086)
*Baobing Zhang,Wanxin Sui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GETA-3DGS是首个端到端自动联合结构化剪枝与量化框架，用于3D高斯泼溅压缩，在不依赖手工阈值的情况下实现约5倍存储压缩，并支持异构混合精度分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS压缩方法（如HAC++、FlexGaussian、LP-3DGS）将剪枝、量化和熵编码视为独立阶段，依赖手工调参的启发式规则（如不透明度阈值、固定位宽、球谐截断），跨场景泛化能力有限，且无法让用户指定目标码率或质量预算。

Method: (i) 构建3DGS感知的量化感知依赖图(QADG)，将每个高斯基元视为一个组，包含五个属性子节点和度感知的球谐子节点；(ii) 提出渲染感知的显著性融合方法，结合透射率加权贡献、屏幕空间梯度和像素覆盖度，计算基元级重要性分数；(iii) 设计异构逐属性混合精度方案，在投影部分显著性引导(PPSG)下降保证下与结构稀疏性联合优化。

Result: 在Mip-NeRF 360、Tanks and Temples和Deep Blending数据集上，GETA-3DSS直接操作原始高斯基元而非后处理锚点表示，相比Vanilla 3DGS实现约5倍存储压缩，无需逐场景阈值。均匀6位量化在视相关场景上损失高达-6.74 dB，而异构分配更优，匹配信息论逆向注水分析。

Conclusion: GETA-3DGS与现有编解码器（如HAC++、CompGS）互补，熵编码作为下游阶段，两者可组合使用。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) is a state-of-the-art representation for real-time photorealistic novel-view synthesis, yet a single high-fidelity scene typically occupies hundreds of megabytes to several gigabytes, exceeding the budgets of mobile, immersive, and volumetric video platforms. Existing 3DGS compression methods (e.g., HAC++, FlexGaussian, LP-3DGS) treat pruning, quantization, and entropy coding as separate stages and rely on hand-tuned heuristics (opacity thresholds, fixed bit-widths, SH truncation), limiting cross-scene generalization and preventing users from specifying a target rate or quality budget. We propose GETA-3DGS, to our knowledge the first end-to-end automatic joint structured pruning and quantization framework for 3DGS. Building on GETA for joint pruning-quantization of deep networks, we contribute: (i) a 3DGS-aware quantization-aware dependency graph (QADG) treating each Gaussian primitive as a group with five attribute sub-nodes and degree-aware SH sub-nodes; (ii) a render-aware saliency fusing transmittance-weighted contribution, screen-space gradient, and pixel coverage into a Gaussian-level importance score; and (iii) a heterogeneous per-attribute mixed-precision scheme co-optimized with structural sparsity under a projected partial saliency-guided (PPSG) descent guarantee. On Mip-NeRF 360, Tanks and Temples, and Deep Blending, GETA-3DGS operates directly on raw Gaussian primitives rather than a post-hoc anchor representation, delivering ~5x storage reduction over Vanilla 3DGS with no per-scene thresholds. Bit-width policy is the dominant rate-distortion lever: a uniform 6-bit cap costs up to -6.74 dB on view-dependent scenes versus our heterogeneous allocation, matching an information-theoretic reverse-water-filling analysis we develop. GETA-3DGS is complementary to existing codecs: entropy coding (HAC++, CompGS) is downstream, so the two can be composed.

</details>


### [90] [Bridging the Gap Between Average and Discounted TD Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02103)
*Haoxing Tian,Zaiwei Chen,Ioannis Ch. Paschalidis,Alex Olshevsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向平均奖励设置下策略评估的TD学习算法，通过双马尔可夫轨迹采样，克服了贝尔曼算子非压缩性带来的理论困难，实现了与折扣设置相匹配的收敛保证和样本效率（条件数从四次方降至二次方）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均奖励设置下的TD学习面临贝尔曼算子非压缩性的根本性理论困难，现有方法均存在局限性，亟需一种能克服这些限制且收敛分析统一适用于线性函数逼近和表格设置的方法。

Method: 提出一种利用两条马尔可夫轨迹采样进行策略评估的新算法，确保收敛到定义良好的投影贝尔曼方程的唯一解。

Result: 收敛分析统一适用于线性函数逼近和表格设置，收敛界不含显式维度相关项；将样本复杂度从四次方降至二次方，与折扣设置相匹配。

Conclusion: 所提算法在平均奖励设置下成功克服了先前理论方法的局限性，实现了与折扣设置相媲美的理论保证和效率，填补了该领域的重要理论空白。

Abstract: The analysis of Temporal Difference (TD) learning in the average-reward setting faces notable theoretical difficulties because the Bellman operator is not contractive with respect to any norm. This complicates standard analyses of stochastic updates that are effective in discounted settings. Although a considerable body of literature addresses these challenges, existing theoretical approaches come with limitations. We introduce a novel algorithm designed explicitly for policy evaluation in the average-reward setting, utilizing sampling from two Markovian trajectories. Our proposed method overcomes previous limitations by guaranteeing convergence to the unique solution of a properly defined projected Bellman equation. Notably, and in contrast to earlier work, our convergence analysis is uniformly applicable to both linear function approximation and tabular settings and does not involve explicit dimension-dependent terms in its convergence bounds. These results align with what is known to hold in the discounted setting. Furthermore, our algorithm achieves improved dependence on the problem's condition number, reducing the sample complexity from quartic, as in prior literature, to quadratic scaling, and thus matching the efficiency seen in the discounted setting.

</details>


### [91] [Sharpness-Aware Pretraining Mitigates Catastrophic Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02105)
*Ishaan Watts,Catherine Li,Sachin Goyal,Jacob Mitchell Springer,Aditi Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，预训练优化器对平坦极小值的偏向（如SAM、大学习率、缩短学习率退火期）能显著提升下游任务性能并减少遗忘，在20M-150M参数规模以及1B参数模型中均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预训练优化只追求最强基模型，忽视了基模型几何特性对后续参数更新中能力保留的影响。作者旨在探索能够优化到更平坦极小值的预训练方法，以改善下游微调和量化后的性能保持。

Method: 研究三种偏向平坦极小值的预训练优化方法：Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM)、大学习率、缩短学习率退火期。在20M-150M参数模型上进行实验，并在OLMo-2-1B检查点中应用短周期SAM中训练阶段。

Result: 这些干预方法在五个常见数据集的后训练中持续提升下游性能，遗忘最多减少80%。在1B规模上，短周期SAM中训练使MetaMath后训练遗忘减少31%，4-bit量化遗忘减少40%。

Conclusion: 预训练中优化到平坦极小值能够有效提升模型在后训练和量化中的能力保留，是一种实用且可扩展的策略。

Abstract: Pretraining optimizers are tuned to produce the strongest possible base model, on the assumption that a stronger starting point yields a stronger model after subsequent changes like post-training and quantization. This overlooks the geometry of the base model which controls how much of the base model's capabilities survive subsequent parameter updates. We study three pretraining optimization approaches that bias optimization toward flatter minima: Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM), large learning rates, and shortened learning rate annealing periods. Across model sizes ranging from 20M to 150M parameters, we find that these interventions consistently improve downstream performance after post-training on five common datasets with up to 80% less forgetting. These principles hold at scale: a short SAM mid-training phase applied to an existing OLMo-2-1B checkpoint reduces forgetting by 31% after MetaMath post-training and by 40% after 4-bit quantization.

</details>


### [92] [Statistical Consistency and Generalization of Contrastive Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02116)
*Yuanfan Li,Xiyuan Wei,Tianbao Yang,Yiming Ying*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为对比表示学习（CRL）建立了统一的统计学习理论，证明对比损失在排序任务中具有统计一致性，并推导出随负样本数增加而改善的泛化界（监督：O(1/m+1/√n)，自监督：O(1/√m+1/√n)），解决了现有理论中负样本越多泛化界越差的矛盾，在大规模视觉-语言模型上得到实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CRL理论存在三个关键缺陷：(i) 统计一致性理解不足；(ii) 泛化界随负样本数增加而恶化，与实证相悖；(iii) 检索性能缺乏理论关注。本文旨在填补这些空白，建立统一的CRL统计学习理论。

Method: 采用AUC型群体指标评估下游检索质量，证明对比损失与最优排序统计一致；建立校准型不等式关联对比风险与检索次优性；分别对监督和自监督对比目标推导泛化界，揭示负样本数m与锚点数n之间的显式权衡。

Result: 监督CRL泛化界为O(1/m+1/√n)，自监督为O(1/√m+1/√n)，表明增加负样本可改善泛化；理论预测在大型视觉-语言模型上得到实验验证。

Conclusion: 本文解决了CRL理论中长期存在的三个难题，不仅解释了大规模负样本的实证优势，还揭示了m与n的显式权衡，为理解对比学习提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Contrastive representation learning (CRL) underpins many modern foundation models. Despite recent theoretical progress, existing analyses suffer from several key limitations: (i) the statistical consistency of CRL remains poorly understood; (ii) available generalization bounds deteriorate as the number of negative samples increases, contradicting the empirical benefits of large negative sets; and (iii) the retrieval performance of CRL has received limited theoretical attention.
  In this paper, we develop a unified statistical learning theory for CRL. For downstream tasks, we evaluate retrieval quality using an AUC-type population criterion and show that the contrastive loss is \emph{statistically consistent} with optimal ranking. We further establish a \emph{calibration-style inequality} that quantitatively relates excess contrastive risk to excess retrieval suboptimality. For upstream training, we study both supervised and self-supervised contrastive objectives and derive generalization bounds of order $O(1/m + 1/\sqrt{n})$ and $O(1/\sqrt{m} + 1/\sqrt{n})$, respectively, where $m$ denotes the number of negative samples and $n$ the number of anchor points. These bounds not only explain the empirical advantages of large negative sets but also reveal an explicit trade-off between $m$ and $n$. Extensive experiments on large-scale vision--language models corroborate our theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [93] [STABLEVAL: Disagreement-Aware and Stable Evaluation of AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02122)
*Akash Bonagiri,Gerard Janno Anderias,Saee Patil,Angelina Lai,Devang Borkar,Gezheng Kang,Ishant Gandhi,Setareh Rafatirad,Houman Homayoun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STABLEVAL框架，通过建模标注者混淆模式与项目潜在正确性来替代多数投票，在标注者异质性和对抗噪声下实现更稳定的系统排名评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数投票聚合方式丢弃了标注者可靠性和项目级歧义信息，导致系统排名在标注者子集间不稳定，需要一种不确定性感知的评估框架来提升评估的鲁棒性和可复现性。

Method: STABLEVAL通过贝叶斯方法建模潜在项目正确性和标注者特定的混淆模式，生成后验期望的项目得分和校准的智能体级评分，不同于Dawid-Skene等标签去噪方法，STABLEVAL专为稳定且不确定性感知的系统评估设计，并将排名稳定性作为第一类评估目标。

Result: 在合成实验和多个真实人类标注基准上，多数投票在标注者异质性和对抗噪声下表现出分数误差增大和排名不稳定，而STABLEVAL产生更稳定且统计上更合理的系统排名。

Conclusion: 对标注者分歧进行显式建模对于实现鲁棒和可复现的AI评估至关重要。

Abstract: Human evaluation remains the primary standard for assessing modern AI systems, yet annotator disagreement, bias, and variability make system rankings fragile under standard majority vote aggregation. Majority vote discards annotator reliability and item-level ambiguity, often yielding unstable comparisons across annotator subsets. We introduce STABLEVAL, a disagreement-aware evaluation framework that models latent item correctness and annotator-specific confusion patterns to produce posterior expected item credit and calibrated agent-level scores. Unlike label-denoising approaches such as Dawid-Skene, STABLEVAL is explicitly designed for stable and uncertainty-aware system evaluation rather than hard label recovery. We formalize ranking stability as a first-class evaluation objective and analyze how aggregation methods preserve or distort underlying annotator behavior. Across controlled synthetic experiments and multiple real-world human-annotated benchmarks, majority vote exhibits increasing score error and ranking instability under annotator heterogeneity and adversarial noise, while STABLEVAL yields more stable and statistically grounded system rankings. These results demonstrate that modeling disagreement is essential for robust and reproducible AI evaluation.

</details>


### [94] [Boundary Mass and the Soft-to-Hard Limit in Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02124)
*Reza Rastegar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Softmax路由混合专家模型在温度趋近于零时的奇异极限行为，证明该极限由路由界面附近的薄几何层控制，并据此推导了软到硬的风险界、Γ-收敛性以及教师-学生框架下的景观传递原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Softmax路由MoE在低温极限下从软路由到硬路由的过渡机制，揭示该极限的奇异性质（路由平局附近的不连续性）及其对模型风险收敛和优化景观的影响。

Method: 在总体水平上分析平方损失MoE回归。定义"边界质量"（top两个路由器分数差距很小的概率），利用余面积公式/管状邻域估计证明该质量与板宽度线性相关（常数由路由界面上的面积分给出）。基于此导出定量软到硬风险界，在紧致性和均匀间隔控制下证明软目标函数Γ-收敛到硬路由目标函数。在教师-学生场景下证明条件性景观传递原理，并通过两专家高斯算例展示局部对称破缺机制。

Result: 零温极限由路由界面周围的薄几何层控制，而非整个输入空间；边界质量关于板宽度呈线性关系；软目标函数在紧致性和均匀间隔控制下Γ-收敛到硬路由目标函数；在适当条件下，低温软路由能继承硬路由问题的近似教师恢复和严格鞍点行为；两专家高斯算例揭示了与教师分割器对齐的局部对称破缺机制。

Conclusion: 软到硬路由的过渡由路由界面附近的几何边界层主导，这为理解MoE模型中温度参数的调节作用提供了严格的定量刻画，并为分析和设计更高效的混合专家模型奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Softmax-routed mixture-of-experts models approach hard routing as the temperature tends to zero, but this limit is singular near routing ties. This paper studies that singularity at the population level for squared-loss MoE regression. The central object is the \emph{boundary mass}, namely the probability that the top two router scores are separated by only a small margin. Under smoothness and transversality assumptions on the router and input law, we prove coarea/tube estimates showing that this mass is linear in the slab width, with leading constant given by a surface integral over the routing interface in the binary case. These estimates yield quantitative soft-to-hard risk bounds and, under compactness and uniform margin control, $Γ$-convergence of the soft objectives to the hard-routing objective. The main conclusion is that the zero-temperature limit is controlled by a thin geometric layer around routing interfaces, not by the full input space. We then use this geometric core in two more model-dependent directions. In a teacher--student setting, we prove a conditional landscape-transfer principle showing that, when the profiled hard-routing problem has favorable identifiability and curvature and the relevant derivatives transfer at boundary-layer scale, small-temperature soft routing inherits approximate teacher recovery and strict-saddle behavior away from teacher-equivalent partitions. We also give a reduced two-expert Gaussian calculation that illustrates a local symmetry-breaking mechanism aligned with the teacher separator.

</details>


### [95] [On the Optimal Sample Complexity of Offline Multi-Armed Bandits with KL Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02141)
*Kaixuan Ji,Qiwei Di,Heyang Zhao,Qingyue Zhao,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了KL正则化离线多臂老虎机的样本复杂度，给出了KL-PCB算法的上界和下界，实现了对正则化强度全范围的近乎完整的刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KL正则化在离线决策中广泛应用，但其离线学习的精确样本复杂度尚未完全刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文在MABs设定下对KL-PCB算法进行锐利分析，分别推导了大正则化（η=Õ(ε^{-1})）和小正则化（η=Ω̃(ε^{-1})）下的样本复杂度上界，并给出了匹配的下界。

Result: 大正则化下样本复杂度为Õ(ηSAC^{π^*}/ε)，小正则化下为Ω̃(SAC^{π^*}/ε^2)，且下界与上界在整个正则化强度范围内匹配。

Conclusion: 本文提供了KL正则化离线多臂老虎机样本复杂度的近乎完整刻画。

Abstract: Kullback-Leibler (KL) regularization is widely used in offline decision-making and offers several benefits, motivating recent work on the sample complexity of offline learning with respect to KL-regularized performance metrics. Nevertheless, the exact sample complexity of KL-regularized offline learning remains largely from fully characterized. In this paper, we study this question in the setting of multi-armed bandits (MABs). We provide a sharp analysis of KL-PCB (Zhao et al., 2026), showing that it achieves a sample complexity of $\tilde{O}(ηSAC^{π^*}/ε)$ under large regularization $η= \tilde{O}(ε^{-1})$, and a sample complexity of $\tildeΩ(SAC^{π^*}/ε^2)$ under small regularization $η= \tildeΩ(ε^{-1})$, where $η$ is the regularization parameter, $S$ is the number of contexts, $A$ is the number of arms, $C^{π^*}$ policy coverage coefficient at the optimal policy $π^*$, $ε$ is the desired sub-optimality, and $\tilde{O}$ and $\tildeΩ$ hide all poly-logarithmic factors. We further provide a pair of sharper sample complexity lower bounds, which matches the upper bounds over the entire range of regularization strengths. Overall, our results provide a nearly complete characterization of offline multi-armed bandits with KL regularization.

</details>


### [96] [Personalized Federated Learning for Gradient Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02143)
*Dongwon Kim,Gyuejeong Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: pFLAlign是一个梯度对齐框架，通过在局部训练中自适应降低梯度方差、在聚合阶段重新对齐全局模型与客户端个性化方向，从而在联邦学习中可靠地保留客户端特定信息，取得了SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有pFL方法难以可靠地保留个性化信息：局部训练受限于有限且异构的数据导致高方差梯度，聚合过程进一步扭曲了客户端的个性化优化方向。

Method: 提出pFLAlign框架，包含两个互补机制：(1) 自适应调整局部梯度方向以降低客户端优化中的方差；(2) 通过重新对齐全局模型与每个客户端的个性化方向来缓解聚合导致的扭曲。理论推导基于PAC贝叶斯分析。

Result: 实验和消融研究一致表明pFLAlign在个性化性能和训练稳定性上均有提升，达到了SOTA结果。

Conclusion: pFLAlign通过梯度对齐有效保留了客户端特定信息，改善了联邦学习中的个性化效果与训练稳定性。

Abstract: Personalized federated learning (pFL) aims to adapt models to client specific data distributions, yet it often fails to reliably preserve personalized information. Local training is hindered by high variance gradients induced by limited and heterogeneous client data, while aggregation further distorts client specific optimization directions. To address these challenges, we propose pFLAlign, a gradient alignment framework to maintain client specific information during both local training and aggregation. pFLAlign consists of two complementary mechanisms: one adapts local gradient directions to reduce variance during client side optimization, and the other mitigates aggregation induced distortion by realigning the global model with each client's personalized direction. Theoretically, we derive pFLAlign from a PAC Bayesian analysis, which reveals how personalized gradient alignment preserves client specific information. Our experiments and ablation studies show that pFLAlign consistently improves personalization performance and training stability, achieving state of the art results.

</details>


### [97] [Projection-Free Transformers via Gaussian Kernel Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02144)
*Debarshi Kundu,Archisman Ghosh,Swaroop Ghosh,Vasant Honavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出高斯核注意力（GKA），用径向基函数核替代标准Transformer中的点积注意力，每个头仅学习一个带宽参数，参数和计算量大幅降低，在语言建模和视觉任务上表现出有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer中的线性投影（Q、K、V）是否必要，能否被更简单的基于相似性的扩散算子替代，以简化注意力机制并提升可解释性。

Method: 提出GKA：使用高斯径向基函数（RBF）核直接计算token间的亲和度，每个注意力头只学习一个带宽参数σ_h，保留单个输出投影W_O以兼容标准Transformer接口。支持因果掩码和滑动窗口约束，可通过掩码和重新归一化实现。

Result: 在深度20的nanochat框架中，GKA模型仅用标准注意力基线0.42倍的参数量和0.49倍的训练FLOPs即可稳定训练，训练-验证差距近乎为零，在标准benchmark上表现出有竞争力的行为，但在此计算规模下bits-per-byte（BPB）较高。

Conclusion: GKA提供了一种极简、可解释的注意力机制，具有显式的局部性尺度，在准确率-效率权衡中提供了新的设计维度，将现代Transformer与经典非局部滤波和核平滑方法联系起来。

Abstract: Self-attention in Transformers is typically implemented as $\mathrm{softmax}(QK^\top/\sqrt{d})V$, where $Q=XW_Q$, $K=XW_K$, and $V=XW_V$ are learned linear projections of the input $X$. We ask whether these learned projections are necessary, or whether they can be replaced by a simpler similarity-based diffusion operator. We introduce \textbf{Gaussian Kernel Attention} (GKA), a drop-in replacement for dot-product attention that computes token affinities directly using a Gaussian radial basis function (RBF) kernel applied to per-head token features. Each head learns only a bandwidth parameter $σ_h$, while a single output projection $W_O$ preserves compatibility with the standard Transformer interface. GKA can be interpreted as normalized kernel regression over tokens, linking modern Transformer architectures to classical non-local filtering and kernel smoothing methods. We evaluate GKA in both vision and language modeling settings. For autoregressive language modeling within the \texttt{nanochat} framework, we implement causal masking and sliding-window constraints by masking and renormalizing the Gaussian kernel. At depth 20, a GKA model with $0.42\times$ the parameters and $0.49\times$ the total training FLOPs of a standard attention baseline trains stably, exhibits a near-zero train-validation gap, and demonstrates competitive behavior on standard benchmarks, albeit with higher bits-per-byte (BPB) at this compute scale. Overall, GKA provides a minimal, interpretable attention mechanism with an explicit locality scale, offering a dimension in the accuracy-efficiency trade-off for Transformer design.

</details>


### [98] [Experience Constrained Hierarchical Federated Reinforcement Learning for Large-scale UAV Teams in Hazardous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02165)
*Qinwei Huang,Rui Zuo,Simon Khan,Qinru Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出经验约束分层联邦强化学习（EC-HFRL）框架，证明在无人机团队的联邦强化学习中，学习者参与度并非越多越好，学习性能主要由经验重用策略和梯度过渡经验主导，而非联邦聚合效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习假设更多学习者参与能提升训练性能，但在无人机团队危险环境下的联邦强化学习中，经验生成受到安全、能量和任务时长等严重约束，该假设可能不成立。因此需要重新审视学习者参与度在经验约束FRL中的作用。

Method: 提出EC-HFRL框架：集群作为联邦学习智能体，内部多个学习者作为并行学习资源，共享同一经验池。通过分析小批量大小决定有效经验重放暴露程度，以及集群内高参与度增加重用水平来研究学习性能。

Result: 增加参与度并不一定提升学习性能。学习性能与经验重用策略及集群内梯度过渡经验的主导性密切相关。小批量大小主要决定有效重放暴露，而更高的集群内参与度增加了重用水平。性能区间主要由学习信号结构决定，而非联邦聚合效应。

Conclusion: 学习者在经验约束FRL中的作用是有限且次要的，推翻了传统联邦学习中"更多参与更好"的假设，为无人机团队在经验受限环境下的联邦强化学习设计提供了新视角。

Abstract: Conventional federated learning assumes that greater learner participation improves training performance, by leveraging abundant, independently generated local data. However, in federated reinforcement learning (FRL) for unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) teams in hazardous environments where experience generation is severely constrained by safety considerations, energy limitations, and mission duration, this assumption may break. This work introduces Experience-Constrained Hierarchical Federated Reinforcement Learning (EC-HFRL), a framework in which clusters act as federated learning agents, while multiple intra-cluster learners represent parallel learning resources that reuse a shared experience pool. We show that increasing participation does not necessarily improve learning performance. Instead, learning performance is strongly associated with experience reuse strategy and the dominance of key analytically identified gradient transition experiences within a cluster. In particular, minibatch size primarily determines effective replay exposure, while higher intra-cluster participation increases reuse level. Empirical results demonstrate that the performance regimes are strongly associated with the structure of the learning signal, rather than federated aggregation effects, clarifying the limited and secondary role of learner participation in experience-constrained FRL.

</details>


### [99] [Manifold-Aligned Guided Integrated Gradients for Reliable Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02167)
*Soyeon Kim,Seongwoo Lim,Kyowoon Lee,Jaesik Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MA-GIG方法，通过在预训练变分自编码器的潜空间中构建归因路径，解决Integrated Gradients在梯度噪声区域产生不可靠解释的问题，该方法能生成更忠实于输入数据的特征归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Integrated Gradients在基线与输入之间的积分路径穿过梯度噪声区域时会产生不可靠的解释；Guided Integrated Gradients虽通过自适应更新低梯度幅度特征减少敏感性，但输入空间引导仍会导致中间输入偏离数据流形。

Method: 提出Manifold-Aligned Guided Integrated Gradients (MA-GIG)，在预训练变分自编码器的潜空间中构建归因路径，通过对中间潜状态解码，使路径偏向学习到的生成流形，减少暴露于不合理的输入空间区域。

Result: MA-GIG通过聚合靠近输入路径特征上的梯度，产生更忠实的解释，减少了离流形噪声，并在多个数据集和分类器上优于先前的基于路径的归因方法。

Conclusion: MA-GIG通过潜空间流形对齐的方式有效解决了IG及其变体的路径噪声问题，生成了更可靠的归因解释。

Abstract: Feature attribution is central to diagnosing and trusting deep neural networks, and Integrated Gradients (IG) is widely used due to its axiomatic properties. However, IG can yield unreliable explanations when the integration path between a baseline and the input passes through regions with noisy gradients. While Guided Integrated Gradients reduces this sensitivity by adaptively updating low-gradient-magnitude features, input-space guidance still produces intermediate inputs that deviate from the data manifold. To address this limitation, we propose \emph{Manifold-Aligned Guided Integrated Gradients} (MA-GIG), which constructs attribution paths in the latent space of a pre-trained variational autoencoder. By decoding intermediate latent states, MA-GIG biases the path toward the learned generative manifold and reduces exposure to implausible input-space regions. Through qualitative and quantitative evaluations, we demonstrate that MA-GIG produces faithful explanations by aggregating gradients on path features proximal to the input. Consequently, our method reduces off-manifold noise and outperforms prior path-based attribution methods across multiple datasets and classifiers. Our code is available at https://github.com/leekwoon/ma-gig/.

</details>


### [100] [Manifold-Constrained Adversarial Training for Long-Tailed Robustness via Geometric Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02183)
*Guanmeng Xian,Ning Yang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出流形约束对抗训练(MCAT)，通过惩罚特征空间中偏离类条件流形的行为并引入ETF正则化，提升长尾分布下的对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准对抗训练在平衡数据集上有效，但在长尾类分布下尾部类鲁棒性严重退化，决策边界不稳定。

Method: MCAT统一框架包含两部分：1) 流形约束项，惩罚对抗样本偏离类条件流形以保持语义有效性；2) ETF正则化，促进类间平衡的几何分离。理论上建立了几何分离与鲁棒边界的下界关系，并证明流形约束的对抗风险上界限制了高密度语义区域的鲁棒风险。

Result: 在标准长尾基准上，MCAT在总体鲁棒性、平衡鲁棒性和尾部类鲁棒性上均取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 流形约束与ETF正则化的联合优化能有效缓解长尾分布下的对抗鲁棒性退化问题。

Abstract: Adversarial training is effective on balanced datasets, but its robustness degrades under longtailed class distributions, where tail classes suffer high robust error and unstable decision boundaries. We propose Manifold-Constrained Adversarial Training (MCAT), a unified framework that enforces the semantic validity of adversarial examples by penalizing deviations from class-conditional manifolds in feature space, while promoting balanced geometric separation across classes via an ETF-inspired regularization. We provide theoretical results that link geometric separation to lower bounds on adversarially robust margins, and show that manifold-constrained adversarial risk upperbounds robust risk on high-density semantic regions. Extensive experiments on standard longtailed benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in overall, balanced, and tail-class adversarial robustness.

</details>


### [101] [DurableUn: Quantization-Induced Recovery Attacks in Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02196)
*Abdullah Ahmad Khan,Ferdous Sohel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现LLM在INT4量化部署下会系统性恢复已遗忘内容（量化恢复攻击QRA），并提出Sharpness-Aware Forgetting方法DURABLEUN-SAF来解决FA-RA-Q-INT4三元悖论，实现稳定的量化鲁棒遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘评估假设训练和部署精度一致，忽视了生产环境中的低比特量化部署（如INT4），导致通过合规审计的模型在实际部署中可能恢复被遗忘数据。

Method: 提出DURABLEUN-SAF（Sharpness-Aware Forgetting），一种面向量化的遗忘目标函数，利用直通估计器（STE）通过INT4舍入传播梯度，实现量化感知训练。

Result: INT4量化导致遗忘内容恢复高达22倍；DURABLEUN-SAF是唯一在BF16、INT8、INT4下均稳定达到耐久性证书的方法（Q-INT4=0.043±0.002，认证率3/3），而SalUn在其已发布超参数下认证率仅1/3。

Conclusion: 发现FA-RA-Q-INT4三元悖论（无法同时实现强遗忘、高实用性和量化鲁棒性），建议将Q-INT4作为与FA、RA并列的标准评估指标。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove specified training data to satisfy privacy regulations such as GDPR. However, existing evaluations assume identical precision at unlearning and deployment, overlooking that production LLMs are deployed at low-bit precision. We show that INT4 quantization systematically restores forgotten content even when models pass compliance audits at bfloat16 (BF16), we term this the quantization recovery attack (QRA). We conduct the first systematic study of unlearning robustness under adapter-space INT4 quantization in the NF4+LoRA regime, evaluating seven methods on LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct across TOFU, MUSE-News, and WikiBio-WPU. INT8 is benign; INT4 induces recovery of up to 22x, worsening with dataset difficulty. We identify the FA-RA-Q-INT4 trilemma: no method simultaneously achieves strong forgetting, high utility, and quantization robustness. A dense Pareto sweep reveals a sharp phase transition once robustness is achieved, retaining accuracy collapses regardless of further tuning. To address this, we propose DURABLEUN-SAF (Sharpness-Aware Forgetting), a quantization-aware objective using Straight-Through Estimator gradients through INT4 rounding. DURABLEUN-SAF is the only method to achieve a stable empirical (0.047, {BF16, INT8, INT4})- durability certificate: Q-INT4= 0.043 +- 0.002, cert rate= 3/3, versus SalUn's cert rate= 1/3 at its own published hyperparameters. We call for Q-INT4 to be adopted as a standard evaluation metric alongside FA and RA.

</details>


### [102] [Break the Block: Dynamic-size Reasoning Blocks for Diffusion Large Language Models via Monotonic Entropy Descent with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02263)
*Yan Jiang,Ruihong Qiu,Zi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出b1框架，通过单调熵下降目标结合强化学习，为扩散大语言模型学习动态推理块大小，取代固定块生成方式，提升推理连贯性和效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散大语言模型采用固定大小的块式半自回归生成，存在两个问题：1）不同推理任务对应不同的最优解码块大小，固定大小无法适配；2）即使在同一任务内，固定块划分会破坏逻辑流，降低推理连贯性。

Method: 提出b1后训练框架，利用块级熵的实证观察（正确推理呈一致下降趋势，错误推理呈波动趋势），设计单调熵下降目标，通过强化学习为扩散大语言模型学习动态大小推理块，可作为即插即用模块集成到现有后训练算法中。

Result: 在多个推理基准上的实验表明，b1相比现有固定块基线取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 动态大小的推理块能够有效提升扩散大语言模型的推理连贯性和效果，验证了基于熵趋势指导块大小学习的可行性。

Abstract: Recent diffusion large language models (dLLMs) have demonstrated both effectiveness and efficiency in reasoning via a block-based semi-autoregressive generation paradigm. Despite their progress, the fixed-size block generations remain a critical bottleneck for effective and coherent reasoning. 1. From a global perspective, different reasoning tasks would correspond to different optimal decoding block sizes, which makes a ``one-size-fits-all'' assumption ineffective. 2. Even within a single reasoning task, the rigid block partitioning would break the logical flow and reduce reasoning coherence. Through empirical observations, we reveal that for block-wise entropy, incorrect reasoning exhibits a fluctuating and unsteady trend between blocks, whereas the correctly generated tasks follow a consistent descending trend. Therefore, this paper proposes b1, a novel post-training framework for dLLMs that learns dynamic-size reasoning blocks via a Monotonic Entropy Descent objective with reinforcement learning to enhance reasoning coherence.b1 integrates seamlessly as a plug-and-play module with existing dLLM's post-training algorithms. Extensive experiments across various reasoning benchmarks showcase b1's consistent improvement over existing fixed-size block baselines. Our code has been released at https://github.com/YanJiangJerry/Block-R1.

</details>


### [103] [HELIX: Hybrid Encoding with Learnable Identity and Cross-dimensional Synthesis for Time Series Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02278)
*Fengming Zhang,Wenjie Du,Huan Zhang,Ke Yu,Shen Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HELIX通过为每个特征分配可学习的特征身份嵌入（persistent feature identity），实现端到端学习任意特征依赖关系，在时间序列插补任务中超越16个基线模型，并在21个实验设置中取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力的时间序列插补方法在每一层重新发现特征关系，缺乏持久锚点来维持一致的表示；而基于图的方法依赖预定义拓扑且假设同质空间关系，无法处理混合空间位置与语义变量的复杂数据集。

Method: 提出HELIX框架，为每个特征分配可学习的持久特征身份嵌入（feature identity），通过端到端方式从时间共变中学习任意特征依赖关系，并结合混合时间-特征注意力机制（hybrid temporal-feature attention）进行插补。

Result: 在5个公共数据集的21个实验设置中，HELIX全面超越16个基线方法，取得最先进性能。机制分析表明，HELIX在不同层逐步将学到的特征身份和依赖关系与潜在物理和语义结构对齐。

Conclusion: HELIX通过持久特征身份嵌入更有效地将跨特征结构转化为插补精度，证明了该方法在处理复杂特征依赖关系中的有效性。

Abstract: Time series imputation benefits from leveraging cross-feature correlations, yet existing attention-based methods re-discover feature relationships at each layer, lacking persistent anchors to maintain consistent representations. To address this, we propose HELIX, which assigns each feature a learnable feature identity, a persistent embedding that captures intrinsic semantic properties throughout the network. Unlike graph-based methods that rely on predefined topology and assume homogeneous spatial relationships, HELIX learns arbitrary feature dependencies end-to-end from temporal co-variation, naturally handling datasets where features mix spatial locations with semantic variables. Integrated with hybrid temporal-feature attention, HELIX achieves the state-of-the-art performance, surpassing all 16 baselines on 5 public datasets across 21 experimental settings in our evaluation. Furthermore, our mechanistic analysis reveals that HELIX aligns learned feature identities and dependencies with latent physical and semantic structure progressively across layers, demonstrating that it more effectively translates cross-feature structure into imputation accuracy.

</details>


### [104] [Graph Federated Unlearning for Privacy Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02297)
*Ruotong Ma,Wentao Yu,Qizhou Wang,Jie Yang,Chen Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对图联邦学习（GFL）中用户退出后隐私泄露的问题，提出了一种融合机器遗忘（MU）的解决方案。通过确保遗忘更新与学习梯度正交以最小化性能影响，并引入由中央服务器维护的虚拟客户端来保留图拓扑和全局嵌入而不恢复已移除实体信息，实现了有效的隐私保护。实验在代表性用户退出场景下证明了该方法的有效性，并超越了七种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GFL中，即使数据以去中心化方式存储，用户退出后其信息仍可能通过全局模型传播至中心服务器并被恶意客户端获取。这种退出后隐私泄露问题在GFL中鲜有关注，而现有GDPR和CCPA等法规赋予用户自由退出系统的权利，因此亟需解决该隐私风险。

Method: 1) 设计正交遗忘更新：确保遗忘更新方向与学习其他数据的梯度方向正交，最小化对整体模型性能的影响。2) 引入虚拟客户端：由中央服务器维护，用于保留图拓扑结构和全局嵌入，同时避免恢复已移除实体的信息。

Result: 在代表性用户退出场景下，该方法有效实现了隐私保护，且整体性能超越了七种当前最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 提出的结合机器遗忘的GFL框架能够有效应对用户退出后的隐私泄露问题，通过正交更新和虚拟客户端机制在保护隐私的同时保持了模型性能，验证了该方法在隐私保持和性能方面的可靠性。

Abstract: Graph federated learning (GFL) facilitates decentralized training on distributed graph data while keeping sensitive user information local, aligning with policies such as GDPR and CCPA that grant users the right to freely join or withdraw from learning systems. However, even decentralized, user information can persist after quitting, potentially propagating to central servers and then redistributing to malicious clients. This privacy leakage during user withdrawal, despite its importance, has received seldom attention in GFL. To fill the gap, we explore the potential of machine unlearning (MU) to thoroughly remove user information. However, classical MU methods are known to degrade overall performance, a problem that is exacerbated in GFL due to local message passing and global model collaboration. To this end, we make two adjustments to mitigate this challenge for GFL. First, we ensure unlearning updates that minimally affect overall performance, steering them in directions orthogonal to the gradients from learning other data. Second, we introduce virtual clients, maintained by the central server, to preserve graph topology and global embeddings without recovering information of removed entities. We conduct comprehensive experiments under a representative user-withdrawal scenario and propose a novel membership inference framework to rigorously evaluate and validate the reliability of our privacy preservation. The experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, which also surpasses the performance of seven state-of-the-art baseline methods.

</details>


### [105] [A Meta Reinforcement Learning Approach to Goals-Based Wealth Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02300)
*Sanjiv R. Das,Harshad Khadilkar,Sukrit Mittal,Daniel Ostrov,Deep Srivastav,Hungjen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于元强化学习（MetaRL）的预训练方法，能在千分之一秒内为新的财富管理问题提供近乎最优的投资组合与目标实现策略，平均期望效用达到最优解的97.8%，且对资本市场制度变化具有鲁棒性，并能处理动态规划无法解决的大规模状态空间问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
